Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the classification object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 593. The moduli space has dimension at most 505 with 5 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 593 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the classification structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 505. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
